# Deck analysis (from bot-match telemetry)

## RRG Dexter Midrange — 69% over 560 games

- **Seat:** in 560 games, this list won 80% on the play and 59% on the draw.
- **Cards most associated with wins:** Yorinobu Arasaka: Steel Dragon (+20 points, drawn in 384 games), Animals Wrecker (+16 points, drawn in 414 games), Meredith Stout: Stone Cold Corpo (+5 points, drawn in 423 games). Games where each was drawn were won that many points more often than games where it was not, over 560 games. This is an association, not proof of value: cards are drawn more in long games, and the bot sells what looks weak.
- **Cards most associated with losses:** Satori: Sword of Saburo (-6 points, drawn in 396 games), Pepe Najarro: Working Doubles (-5 points, drawn in 419 games). Games where each was drawn were won that many points less often than games where it was not, over 560 games. This is an association, not proof of value: cards are drawn more in long games, and the bot sells what looks weak.
- **Usual Eddie fodder:** Detonate (sold in 94% of the 396 games it was drawn), Synapse Burnout (sold in 94% of the 398 games it was drawn), Peace Offering (sold in 87% of the 402 games it was drawn), Mantis Blades (sold in 81% of the 414 games it was drawn). These are the cards the bot sold most often.
- **Tempo check:** after its 4th turn, this list won 74% of the 312 games it was ahead on Gigs and 66% of the 198 games it was behind.
- **Overtime:** 32% of 560 games reached overtime, and this list won 60% of those (69% overall).
- **Mulligans:** the bot mulliganed in 19% of 560 games, and won 61% of those (69% overall).
- **First Legend flipped:** Dexter DeShawn: Off the Grid 71% (187 games) · Saburo Arasaka: Stubborn Patriarch 70% (188 games) · Adam Smasher: Ender of Legends 66% (184 games). Win rate by the first Legend Called; the flip is blind, but these gaps carry no interval, so small ones may be chance.
- **Reactive Calls:** the bot made 15% of its Legend Calls during the rival's attack, over 560 games.
- **Best matchup:** Keep 'Em Guessing (100% over 8 games). **Worst:** Ladder: V / Smasher / Padre (RRG) (25% over 8 games).
